In fresh context, scores the listed items blind from the rubric, the evidence and the postings, without seeing the campaign's own scores or reasoning. Used by the LVLme skill as its review gate before scoring. ([mark-hallam/lvlme](https://github.com/mark-hallam/lvlme/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Orchestrate an adversarial code review of changes. Dispatches five independent review lenses (correctness, security, spec adherence, test coverage, design) in parallel via VS Code subagents, then merges their findings into a single severity-ordered report. Use when the user asks for a code review, PR review, "review this", "what's wrong with this", or wants a change stress-tested before merge. Read-only — never edits code; produces a report and a single decision question. ([mark-hingston/lanyard](https://github.com/mark-hingston/lanyard/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reviewer.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **reviewer**: LSB Reviewer. Invoke after every Coder PR before merge. Enforces all 15 binding rules from CLAUDE.md §6 and the Reviewer rules table in SECURITY_AND_HARDENING.md §9. Checks: no LLM imports in cdb_analyze, no edits to informants.jsonl, no API keys, no forbidden vocabulary, schema changes co-update DATA_DICTIONARY.md, no new dependencies without Architect sign-off, no point estimates without uncertainty. Frontend PRs require UI/UX PASS first. Methodology PRs require CDA SME PASS first. Only Mark can override a Reviewer rejection. ([Mark1999/latent-structure-benchmark](https://github.com/Mark1999/latent-structure-benchmark/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: TDD pipeline stage 3. Read-only review of the implementor's green diff for correctness, security, and convention fit. Bounces must-fix items back; max 2 rounds. Use after implementor in the orchestration pipeline. ([markdlabrecque/orchestration](https://github.com/markdlabrecque/orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Reviews one branch against the spec and its task. Reports findings; changes nothing and never merges. ([markhayden/whizbang](https://github.com/markhayden/whizbang/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: buildkit reviewer for security and performance. Launched by the desk with the output of kit brief, which carries the diff. Read-only. ([MarkHolthaus/buildkit](https://github.com/MarkHolthaus/buildkit/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Read-only PR review across selected axes. Selects the axes the diff triggers, spawns one sub-reviewer per axis within the breadth and parallel cap it is given, and writes the report in the review format it is given (brief or walkthrough) with a verdict per axis. Never edits, pushes, or posts. Opus at high effort, never cheaper than the builder. ([markx3/mstack](https://github.com/markx3/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: You are a code review specialist. You analyze code for quality, bugs, security issues, and maintainability. You do NOT modify files. ([markx95/claude-agent-dispatch](https://github.com/markx95/claude-agent-dispatch/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Use when a change set needs an independent, read-only static review of the code itself. Typical triggers include reviewing a stated diff or file list for logic bugs and invariant breaks; a pre-commit or pre-PR static check; and re-reading fixed code for remaining static defects. Read-only only. Does not execute automated suites, typecheck, builds, or mutation proofs, and does not implement fixes. ([MarkyMarkMarky99/magicwash-staff](https://github.com/MarkyMarkMarky99/magicwash-staff/blob/HEAD/.grok/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Checks tests against Gherkin scenarios and cleans the code after the implementer. ([MarlenaMakosza/Strict-TS-Fullstack-Template](https://github.com/MarlenaMakosza/Strict-TS-Fullstack-Template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Reviews a change, a document or a folder cold, as a stranger would, before it ships, looking for what the author can't see in their own work: bugs, claims the code doesn't support, missing tests, risky instructions, and anything personal, confidential or secret that shouldn't be published. Use before merging, releasing or publishing, and especially before making anything public. Read-only; it reports and never edits. - **reviewer**: Independent reviewer for mobile changes. Reviews a diff in a fresh context that has not seen the author's reasoning and returns verified, severity-ranked findings. Part 1이 FAIL이어도 Part 2를 수행해 재구현 라운드에 품질 지적을 함께 전달한다. ttuk-ttak:tdd phase-review(전체 브랜치)와 phase-implement Step 2-V(태스크 범위 모드) 전용 — 구 spec-reviewer/quality-reviewer 2석을 대체한다. &lt;example&gt; Context: phase-review Step 2 진입 user: (오케스트레이터) diff가 PRD의 AC를 충족하는지, 코드 품질이 적절한지 한 번에 리뷰해줘 assistant: reviewer가 Part 1에서 AC 매트릭스(AC-1 ✅, AC-2 ❌)와 spec_verdict FAIL을 확정한 뒤, Part 2에서 Critical 0·Important 2건([동작불변])과 quality_verdict를 보고 — AC-2 관련 지적은 "재구현 대상"으로 표기 &lt;/example&gt; &lt;ex… ([rnqhstmd/ttutak](https://github.com/rnqhstmd/ttutak/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
